Only `nbody/nbody.cpp` and `nbody/CMakeLists.txt` changed. The executable is still `nbody`, and the build uses CUDA unconditionally.\n\n**What changed**\n- **`nbody.cpp`**: the O(n²) force loop is now a CUDA kernel with one thread per body. Each block stages a tile of positions in shared memory. Positions are double-buffered, so a step never reads a position another thread has already advanced. Bodies are uploaded once before timing and downloaded once afterwards, so the step loop never touches host memory. Initialization, energy, validation, and output are unchanged and still run on the host.\n- **`CMakeLists.txt`**: enables the CUDA language, builds for `native` architectures, and compiles `nbody.cpp` as CUDA via `set_source_files_properties`, so no new file was needed. CMake minimum is now 3.24.\n\n**Verification**\n- I compared against the original CPU code (built with the same FMA setting) across several sizes, including with `-r` hashes. The GPU output matches exactly, including the FNV hash, for n = 300, 1000, and 4096.\n- `-v` validation passes, `-n 0` works, and `-h` and unknown-option handling are unchanged.\n- Timing at n = 16384, 10 steps: about 381 ms on the RTX 3090 versus about 19.9 s for the original CPU code.\n\n**Choices to review**\n- **`--fmad=false`**: this keeps every multiply and add rounded separately, which is what makes the hashes match. Without it the GPU results differ from the CPU in the last few digits. The cost is about 20–25% in speed. If bit-exact output doesn't matter, remove that flag.\n- **One thread per body**: summation order per body is preserved, which is what gives the exact match. Splitting the j-loop across threads would parallelize further for small n, but it changes summation order and so the hashes.\n- **Energy computation stays on the CPU**: it runs only with `-v`, outside the timed region, and moving it to the GPU would change its summation order.\n- **Timing** covers only the simulation kernels (plus the final sync), as the original timed only the compute loop. Transfers and CUDA context setup are excluded.\n\nI did not commit anything.
